Remove common question prefixes/suffixes\n    text = re.sub(r'^(how (do|can|to|would|should|does)|what (is|are)|why (does|is)|where (is|are)|is (it|there)|can (i|you)|do (i|you))\\s+', ' ', text)\n    text = re.sub(r'\\?.*$', '', text)\n    text = re.sub(r'[^\\w\\s]', ' ', text)\n    text = ' '.join(text.split())\n    return text.strip()\n\ndef extract_duplicate_references(body):\n    \"\"\"Extract all duplicate references from question body\"\"\"\n    pattern = r'>\\s*\\*\\*Possible Duplicate:\\*\\*\\s*>([^\\n]+)'\n    matches = re.findall(pattern, body)\n    return [m.strip() for m in matches]\n\ndef find_best_match_by_title(ref_title, candidates):\n    \"\"\"Find the best matching question by title similarity\"\"\"\n    ref_norm = normalize_text(ref_title)\n    ref_partial = normalize_partial(ref_title)\n    \n    best_match = None\n    best_score = 0.3  # {"type":"tool_use","timestamp":1782141954710,"sessionID":"ses_11013b318ffe8emar1GcKXSRJH","part":{"type":"tool","tool":"write","callID":"functions.write:17","state":{"status":"completed","input":{"content":"#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"\nDuplicate Question Clustering Script - Version 4\nFocus on explicit duplicate markers with proper matching and transitive closure.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport json\nimport re\nfrom collections import defaultdict\nfrom difflib import SequenceMatcher\n\ndef load_all_questions():\n    \"\"\"Load all questions from all parts\"\"\"\n    all_questions = []\n    for i in range(1, 13):\n        part_file = f'/input_artifacts/part_{i:02d}.json'\n        with open(part_file, 'r') as f:\n            data = json.load(f)\n            all_questions.extend(data['records'])\n    return all_questions\n\ndef normalize_text(text):\n    \"\"\"Normalize text for comparison\"\"\"\n    text = text.lower()\n    text = re.sub(r'[^\\w\\s]', ' ', text)\n    text = re.sub(r'\\s+', ' ', text).strip()\n    return text\n\ndef extract_duplicate_refs(body):\n    \"\"\"Extract duplicate references from question body\"\"\"\n    pattern = r'>\\s*\\*\\*Possible Duplicate:\\*\\*\\s*>(.+?)(?:\\n|$)'\n    matches = re.findall(pattern, body)\n    return [m.strip() for m in matches]\n\ndef find_question_by_ref(ref_text, forum_questions):\n    \"\"\"Find the best matching question for a reference\"\"\"\n    ref_norm = normalize_text(ref_text)\n    ref_words = set(ref_norm.split())\n    \n    best_match = None\n    best_score = 0.0\n    \n    for q in forum_questions:\n        q_norm = normalize_text(q['title'])\n        \n        # Calculate multiple similarity measures\n        scores = []\n        \n        # 1. Exact or substring match\n        if ref_norm in q_norm or q_norm in ref_norm:\n            min_len = min(len(ref_norm), len(q_norm))\n            max_len = max(len(ref_norm), len(q_norm))\n            scores.append(0.9 + 0.1 * (min_len / max_len))\n        \n        # 2. Word overlap (Jaccard)\n        q_words = set(q_norm.split())\n        if ref_words and q_words:\n            overlap = len(ref_words & q_words) / len(ref_words | q_words)\n            scores.append(overlap)\n        \n        # 3. Sequence similarity\n        seq_sim = SequenceMatcher(None, ref_norm, q_norm).ratio()\n        scores.append(seq_sim)\n        \n        # 4. First 10 chars match (for truncated references)\n        if len(ref_norm) >= 10 and len(q_norm) >= 10:\n            if ref_norm[:10] == q_norm[:10]:\n                scores.append(0.85)\n        \n        if scores:\n            score = max(scores)\n            if score > best_score and score >= 0.5:\n                best_score = score\n                best_match = q\n    \n    return best_match, best_score\n\ndef calculate_similarity(q1, q2):\n    \"\"\"Calculate similarity between two questions for grouping non-explicit duplicates\"\"\"\n    title1 = normalize_text(q1['title'])\n    title2 = normalize_text(q2['title'])\n    body1 = normalize_text(q1['body'])\n    body2 = normalize_text(q2['body'])\n    \n    # Title similarity\n    title_seq = SequenceMatcher(None, title1, title2).ratio()\n    \n    title_words1 = set(title1.split())\n    title_words2 = set(title2.split())\n    if title_words1 and title_words2:\n        title_jaccard = len(title_words1 & title_words2) / len(title_words1 | title_words2)\n    else:\n        title_jaccard = 0\n    \n    # Body similarity (use word overlap)\n    body_words1 = set(body1.split())\n    body_words2 = set(body2.split())\n    if body_words1 and body_words2:\n        body_jaccard = len(body_words1 & body_words2) / len(body_words1 | body_words2)\n    else:\n        body_jaccard = 0\n    \n    # Weighted combination\n    similarity = 0.4 * title_seq + 0.3 * title_jaccard + 0.3 * body_jaccard\n    \n    return similarity\n\ndef build_explicit_duplicate_graph(questions):\n    \"\"\"Build graph from explicit duplicate markers\"\"\"\n    # Group by forum\n    forum_map = defaultdict(list)\n    for q in questions:\n        forum_map[q['tag_forum']].append(q)\n    \n    # Build ID to question map\n    id_map = {q['id']: q for q in questions}\n    \n    # Graph edges\n    edges = defaultdict(set)\n    unresolved = []\n    resolved_count = 0\n    \n    for q in questions:\n        refs = extract_duplicate_refs(q['body'])\n        for ref in refs:\n            target, score = find_question_by_ref(ref, forum_map[q['tag_forum']])\n            \n            if target and target['id'] != q['id']:\n                edges[q['id']].add(target['id'])\n                edges[target['id']].add(q['id'])\n                resolved_count += 1\n            else:\n                unresolved.append((q['id'], ref))\n    \n    print(f\"Resolved {resolved_count} explicit duplicate references\")\n    print(f\"Unresolved: {len(unresolved)}\")\n    \n    return edges, unresolved\n\ndef add_similarity_edges(edges, questions, threshold=0.75):\n    \"\"\"Add edges based on similarity for questions not connected by explicit markers\"\"\"\n    #
